<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_0143yusutrAjHa9WT5geY2ba`</summary>

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nRRF (Reciprocal Rank Fusion) aggregation of contamination signals.\\n\\nFuses 4 per-item signal scores into a single exposure score using RRF\\n(Cormack et al. 2009). Also implements ablation variants: equal-weight\\nrank averaging and single-signal baselines.\\n\\nInput:  Per-item signal scores (JSON from compute_signals.py)\\nOutput: Per-model-item fused exposure scores + signal survival AUC matrix\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSIGNAL_KEYS = [\\\"s1_embedding\\\", \\\"s2_minkpp\\\", \\\"s3_constat\\\", \\\"s4_selfcritique\\\"]\\nRRF_K = 60\\n\\n\\ndef load_signal_data(signal_files: list[str]) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load and merge signal files. Each file contains a list of per-item dicts.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    merged = {}\\n    for fpath in signal_files:\\n        with open(fpath) as f:\\n            data = json.load(f)\\n        for item in data:\\n            idx = item[\\\"index\\\"]\\n            if idx not in merged:\\n                merged[idx] = {\\\"index\\\": idx, \\\"benchmark\\\": item.get(\\\"benchmark\\\", \\\"\\\")}\\n            merged[idx].update(item)\\n    return [merged[k] for k in sorted(merged)]\\n\\n\\ndef compute_ranks(scores: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Rank items from 1 (highest score = most suspicious) to N.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    order = np.argsort(-scores)  # descending\\n    ranks = np.empty_like(order)\\n    ranks[order] = np.arange(1, len(scores) + 1)\\n    return ranks\\n\\n\\ndef rrf_aggregate(items: list[dict], k: int = RRF_K,\\n                  signals: list[str] = None) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    RRF fusion: score_j = sum_s 1/(k + rank_s(j))\\n\\n    All signals are oriented so higher = more suspicious.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if signals is None:\\n        signals = SIGNAL_KEYS\\n\\n    n = len(items)\\n    if n == 0:\\n        return []\\n\\n    # Extract score vectors\\n    score_matrix = {}\\n    for sig in signals:\\n        vals = np.array([item.get(sig, 0.0) or 0.0 for item in items], dtype=float)\\n        score_matrix[sig] = vals\\n\\n    # Compute ranks for each signal\\n    rank_matrix = {}\\n    for sig in signals:\\n        rank_matrix[sig] = compute_ranks(score_matrix[sig])\\n\\n    # RRF fusion\\n    rrf_scores = np.zeros(n)\\n    for sig in signals:\\n        rrf_scores += 1.0 / (k + rank_matrix[sig])\\n\\n    # Build output\\n    results = []\\n    for i, item in enumerate(items):\\n        record = {\\n            \\\"index\\\": item[\\\"index\\\"],\\n            \\\"benchmark\\\": item.get(\\\"benchmark\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n            \\\"rrf_score\\\": float(rrf_scores[i]),\\n        }\\n        for sig in signals:\\n            record[f\\\"rank_{sig}\\\"] = int(rank_matrix[sig][i])\\n        results.append(record)\\n\\n    return results\\n\\n\\ndef rank_average_aggregate(items: list[dict],\\n                           signals: list[str] = None) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Equal-weight rank averaging (ablation baseline).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if signals is None:\\n        signals = SIGNAL_KEYS\\n\\n    n = len(items)\\n    if n == 0:\\n        return []\\n\\n    rank_matrix = {}\\n    for sig in signals:\\n        vals = np.array([item.get(sig, 0.0) or 0.0 for item in items], dtype=float)\\n        rank_matrix[sig] = compute_ranks(vals)\\n\\n    avg_ranks = np.zeros(n)\\n    for sig in signals:\\n        avg_ranks += rank_matrix[sig]\\n    avg_ranks /= len(signals)\\n\\n    # Lower average rank = more suspicious, so score = -avg_rank for consistent ordering\\n    results = []\\n    for i, item in enumerate(items):\\n        results.append({\\n            \\\"index\\\": item[\\\"index\\\"],\\n            \\\"benchmark\\\": item.get(\\\"benchmark\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n            \\\"rank_avg_score\\\": float(-avg_ranks[i]),\\n            \\\"mean_rank\\\": float(avg_ranks[i]),\\n        })\\n    return results\\n\\n\\ndef single_signal_scores(items: list[dict]) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Single-signal ablation: each signal alone as the exposure score.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    results = {}\\n    for sig in SIGNAL_KEYS:\\n        vals = [{\\\"index\\\": item[\\\"index\\\"], \\\"benchmark\\\": item.get(\\\"benchmark\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n                 f\\\"single_{sig}\\\": item.get(sig, 0.0) or 0.0} for item in items]\\n        results[sig] = vals\\n    return results\\n\\n\\ndef compute_signal_auc(items: list[dict], labels: list[int]) -> dict:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Compute per-signal AUC if ground-truth contamination labels are available.\\n    Returns signal survival AUC matrix.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from sklearn.metrics import roc_auc_score\\n\\n    labels_arr = np.array(labels)\\n    if len(np.unique(labels_arr)) < 2:\\n        logger.warning(\\\"Cannot compute AUC: labels have only one class\\\")\\n        return {}\\n\\n    auc_results = {}\\n    for sig in SIGNAL_KEYS:\\n        scores = np.array([item.get(sig, 0.0) or 0.0 for item in items])\\n        try:\\n            auc = roc_auc_score(labels_arr, scores)\\n            auc_results[sig] = float(auc)\\n        except ValueError as e:\\n            logger.warning(\\\"AUC computation failed for %s: %s\\\", sig, e)\\n            auc_results[sig] = None\\n\\n    # Also compute AUC for RRF fused score\\n    rrf_results = rrf_aggregate(items)\\n    rrf_scores = np.array([r[\\\"rrf_score\\\"] for r in rrf_results])\\n    try:\\n        auc_results[\\\"rrf_fused\\\"] = float(roc_auc_score(labels_arr, rrf_scores))\\n    except ValueError:\\n        auc_results[\\\"rrf_fused\\\"] = None\\n\\n    return auc_results\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"RRF aggregation of contamination signals\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--signal-files\\\", type=str, nargs=\\\"+\\\", required=True,\\n                        help=\\\"JSON files with per-item signal scores (or merged signal file)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--output-dir\\\", type=str, required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--k\\\", type=int, default=RRF_K,\\n                        help=\\\"RRF k parameter (default: 60)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--labels-file\\\", type=str, default=None,\\n                        help=\\\"JSON file with ground-truth contamination labels (list of 0/1)\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--model-name\\\", type=str, default=\\\"unknown\\\",\\n                        help=\\\"Model identifier for output naming\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--benchmark\\\", type=str, default=None)\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\")\\n\\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n\\n    # Load signals\\n    items = load_signal_data(args.signal_files)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Loaded %d items with signals\\\", len(items))\\n\\n    # RRF aggregation\\n    rrf_results = rrf_aggregate(items, k=args.k)\\n    rrf_file = output_dir / f\\\"rrf_scores_{args.model_name}.json\\\"\\n    with open(rrf_file, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(rrf_results, f, indent=2)\\n    logger.info(\\\"RRF scores saved to %s\\\", rrf_file)\\n\\n    # Ablation: rank averaging\\n    ra_results = rank_average_aggregate(items)\\n    ra_file = output_dir / f\\\"rank_avg_scores_{args.model_name}.json\\\"\\n    with open(ra_file, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(ra_results, f, indent=2)\\n\\n    # Signal 2: Min-K%++\\n    if \\\"minkpp\\\" in args.signals:\\n        logger.info(\\\"=== Signal 2: Min-K%%++ membership inference ===\\\")\\n        signal_results[\\\"minkpp\\\"] = run_signal(\\\"minkpp\\\", {\\n            \\\"benchmark\\\": args.benchmark,\\n            \\\"target_model_path\\\": args.model_path,\\n            \\\"ref_model_path\\\": args.ref_model_path,\\n            \\\"cache_dir\\\": args.cache_dir,\\n            \\\"k_percent\\\": args.k_percent,\\n        })\\n        _save_signal(signal_results[\\\"minkpp\\\"], output_dir, \\\"minkpp\\\", args.benchmark)\\n\\n    # Signal 3: ConStat\\n    if \\\"constat\\\" in args.signals:\\n        if args.original_eval_paths and args.paraphrase_eval_paths:\\n            logger.info(\\\"=== Signal 3: ConStat performance gap ===\\\")\\n            signal_results[\\\"constat\\\"] = run_signal(\\\"constat\\\", {\\n                \\\"benchmark\\\": args.benchmark,\\n                \\\"original_eval_paths\\\": args.original_eval_paths,\\n                \\\"paraphrase_eval_paths\\\": args.paraphrase_eval_paths,\\n            })\\n            _save_signal(signal_results[\\\"constat\\\"], output_dir, \\\"constat\\\", args.benchmark)\\n        else:\\n            logger.warning(\\\"Skipping ConStat: --original-eval-paths and --paraphrase-eval-paths required\\\")\\n\\n    # Signal 4: Self-Critique\\n    if \\\"selfcritique\\\" in args.signals:\\n        logger.info(\\\"=== Signal 4: Self-Critique entropy collapse ===\\\")\\n        signal_results[\\\"selfcritique\\\"] = run_signal(\\\"selfcritique\\\", {\\n            \\\"benchmark\\\": args.benchmark,\\n            \\\"model_path\\\": args.model_path,\\n            \\\"cache_dir\\\": args.cache_dir,\\n        })\\n        _save_signal(signal_results[\\\"selfcritique\\\"], output_dir, \\\"selfcritique\\\", args.benchmark)\\n\\n    # Merge all computed signals (graceful: merge whenever >=2 signals available)\\n    if len(signal_results) >= 2:\\n        logger.info(\\\"=== Merging all signals ===\\\")\\n        merged = merge_signals(\\n            signal_results.get(\\\"embedding\\\", []),\\n            signal_results.get(\\\"minkpp\\\", []),\\n            signal_results.get(\\\"constat\\\", []),\\n            signal_results.get(\\\"selfcritique\\\", []),\\n            args.benchmark,\\n        )\\n        merged_file = output_dir / f\\\"signals_merged_{args.benchmark}.json\\\"\\n        with open(merged_file, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n            json.dump(merged, f, indent=2)\\n        logger.info(\\\"Merged signals saved to %s (%d items)\\\", merged_file, len(merged))\\n    else:\\n        logger.info(\\\"Computed %d/4 signals. Need at least 2 for merged output.\\\", len(signal_results))\\n\\n\\ndef _save_signal(results: list[dict], output_dir: Path, signal_name: str, benchmark: str):\\n    out_file = output_dir / f\\\"signal_{signal_name}_{benchmark}.json\\\"\\n    with open(out_file, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Saved %d items to %s\\\", len(results), out_file)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.41}}"}
```

```json
      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nexp-002 Stage 1 Signal Pipeline — main entry point.\\n\\nComputes 4 contamination detection signals for each model-item vs paraphrased accuracy)\\n  S4: Self-Critique entropy collapse (YES/NO confidence on own answer)\\n\\nInput:  Model checkpoint, benchmark data, paraphrased items, SFT training data\\nOutput: JSON per model with item_id, s1_embedding, s2_minkpp, s3_constat, s4_selfcritique\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\ndef merge_signals(\\n    embedding_results: list[dict],\\n    minkpp_results: list[dict],\\n    constat_results: list[dict],\\n    selfcritique_results: list[dict],\\n    benchmark: str,\\n) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Merge 4 signal outputs into a single per-item record.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # Index by item index\\n    emb_map = {r[\\\"index\\\"]: r for r in embedding_results}\\n    mkpp_map = {r[\\\"index\\\"]: r for r in minkpp_results}\\n    con_map = {r[\\\"index\\\"]: r for r in constat_results}\\n    sc_map = {r[\\\"index\\\"]: r for r in selfcritique_results}\\n\\n    all_indices = sorted(set(emb_map) | set(mkpp_map) | set(con_map) | set(sc_map))\\n\\n    merged = []\\n    for idx in all_indices:\\n        record = {\\n            \\\"index\\\": idx,\\n            \\\"benchmark\\\": benchmark,\\n            \\\"s1_embedding\\\": emb_map.get(idx, {}).get(\\\"s1_embedding\\\"),\\n            \\\"s2_minkpp\\\": mkpp_map.get(idx, {}).get(\\\"s2_minkpp\\\"),\\n            \\\"s3_constat\\\": con_map.get(idx, {}).get(\\\"s3_constat\\\"),\\n            \\\"s4_selfcritique\\\": sc_map.get(idx, {}).get(\\\"s4_selfcritique\\\"),\\n        }\\n        merged.append(record)\\n\\n    return merged\\n\\n\\ndef run_signal(signal_name: str, args_dict: dict):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Run a signal computation module.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if signal_name == \\\"embedding\\\":\\n        from signal_embedding import compute_embedding_signal\\n        return compute_embedding_signal(**args_dict)\\n    elif signal_name == \\\"minkpp\\\":\\n description=\\\"Compute all 4 contamination detection signals for a model-benchmark pair\\\"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--model-path\\\", type=str, required=True,\\n Execute tasks sequentially\\n    results = {}\\n    for i, task in enumerate(tasks, 1):\\n        task_id = f\\\"{task['model_id']}/{task['benchmark']}\\\"\\n        logger.info(\\\"[%d/%d] Running: %s (signals: %s)\\\", i, len(tasks),\\n                    task_id, task[\\\"signals\\\"])\\n\\n        try:\\n            result = subprocess.run(\\n                task[\\\"cmd\\\"],\\n                cwd=\\\"/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp002\\\",\\n                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=7200,\\n            )\\n            if result.returncode == 0:\\n                results[task_id] = \\\"PASS\\\"\\n                logger.info(\\\"[%d/%d] PASS: %s\\\", i, len(tasks), task_id)\\n            else:\\n                results[task_id] = f\\\"FAIL (rc={result.returncode})\\\"\\n                logger.error(\\\"[%d/%d] FAIL: %s\\\\nstderr: %s\\\", i, len(tasks),\\n                             task_id, result.stderr[-500:])\\n        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\\n            results[task_id] = \\\"TIMEOUT\\\"\\n            logger.error(\\\"[%d/%d] TIMEOUT: %s\\\", i, len(tasks), task_id)\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            results[task_id] = f\\\"ERROR: {e}\\\"\\n            logger.error(\\\"[%d/%d] ERROR: %s — %s\\\", i, len(tasks), task_id, e)\\n\\n    # Summary\\n    logger.info(\\\"\\\\n\\\" + \\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    logger.info(\\\"BATCH SUMMARY\\\")\\n    logger.info(\\\"=\\\" * 60)\\n    for task_id, status in results.items():\\n        marker = \\\"PASS\\\" if status == \\\"PASS\\\" else \\\"FAIL\\\"\\n        logger.info(\\\"  [%s] %s: %s\\\", marker, task_id, status)\\n\\n    n_pass = sum(1 for v in results.values() if v == \\\"PASS\\\")\\n    n_total = len(results)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Result: %d/%d passed\\\", n_pass, n_total)\\n\\n    # Write batch report\\n    report_path = Path(OUTPUT_ROOT) / f\\\"batch_report_{model_name}.json\\\"\\n    report_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    report = {\\n        \\\"model_name\\\": model_name,\\n        \\\"model_size\\\": args.model_size,\\n        \\\"dry_run\\\": args.dry_run,\\n        \\\"tasks_run\\\": n_total,\\n        \\\"tasks_passed\\\": n_pass,\\n        \\\"skipped\\\": skipped,\\n        \\\"results\\\": results,\\n    }\\n    with open(report_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\\n    logger.info(\\\"Batch report saved to %s\\\", report_path)\\n\\n    sys.exit(0 if n_pass == n_total else 1)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.39}}"
```

```json
      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nIRT calibration for contamination-aware benchmark score correction.\\nFits 4PL+gamma IRT model via CmdStanPy MCMC (NUTS) per benchmark.\\n\\nInput:\\n  - Correctness matrix (N_models x N_items): binary 0/1\\n  - Exposure matrix (N_models x N_items): continuous exposure scores\\n  OR\\n  - eval-dir with per-model eval results + per-benchmark exposure scores\\n\\nOutput (per benchmark):\\n  - Posterior: gamma, theta, item params (a, b, c, d)\\n  - Calibrated scores (exposure=0 counterfactual) with 50/80/95% CIs\\n  - Convergence diagnostics (Rhat, ESS, divergences)\\n  - Posterior predictive checks\\n\\nDependencies: cmdstanpy (CmdStan 2.38+), numpy, scipy\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nimport sys\\nimport time\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport cmdstanpy\\n\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSCRIPT_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\\nSTAN_MODEL_DIR = \\\"/root/contamcal-irt-calibration/stan_models\\\"\\nSTAN_MODELS = {\\n    \\\"4pl_gamma\\\": os.path.join(STAN_MODEL_DIR, \\\"contam_irt_4pl_simple.stan\\\"),\\n    \\\"2pl_gamma\\\": os.path.join(STAN_MODEL_DIR, \\\"contam_irt_2pl_gamma.stan\\\"),\\n    \\\"vanilla_4pl\\\": os.path.join(STAN_MODEL_DIR, \\\"vanilla_4pl.stan\\\"),\\n}\\n\\nDOSAGE_PATTERN = re.compile(r\\\"_d(\\\\d+)_s(\\\\d+)(_para)?$\\\")\\n\\n\\n# ── Data Loading ──\\n\\ndef parse_model_dir_name(dirname):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract model name, dosage, seed from directory like Qwen2.5-0.5B_d025_s42.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    m = DOSAGE_PATTERN.search(dirname)\\n    if not m:\\n        return None\\n    return {\\n        \\\"dirname\\\": dirname,\\n        \\\"model_name\\\": dirname[:m.start()],\\n        \\\"dosage\\\": int(m.group(1)),\\n        \\\"seed\\\": int(m.group(2)),\\n        \\\"is_para\\\": m.group(3) is not None,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef load_matrix(path):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load matrix from .npy, .csv, or .json.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    ext = os.path.splitext(path)[1].lower()\\n    if ext == \\\".npy\\\":\\n        return np.load(path)\\n    elif ext == \\\".csv\\\":\\n        return np.loadtxt(path, delimiter=\\\",\\\", skiprows=1)\\n    elif ext == \\\".json\\\":\\n        with open(path) as f:\\n            return np.array(json.load(f))\\n    raise ValueError(f\\\"Unsupported format: {ext}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef load_eval_results(eval_dir, benchmarks=None, skip_para=True):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load per-model eval results into response matrices per benchmark.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    eval_path = Path(eval_dir)\\n    model_dirs = sorted([\\n        d for d in eval_path.iterdir()\\n        if d.is_dir() and (d / \\\"eval_results.json\\\").exists()\\n    ])\\n\\n    models = []\\n    for d in model_dirs:\\n        info = parse_model_dir_name(d.name)\\n        if info is None:\\n            log.warning(\\\"Skipping unrecognized dir: %s\\\", d.name)\\n            continue\\n        if skip_para and info[\\\"is_para\\\"]:\\n            continue\\n        info[\\\"path\\\"] = str(d)\\n        models.append(info)\\n\\n    if not models:\\n        raise ValueError(f\\\"No valid eval results in {eval_dir}\\\")\\n    log.info(\\\"Found %d model evaluations\\\", len(models))\\n\\n    with open(Path(models[0][\\\"path\\\"]) / \\\"eval_results.json\\\") as f:\\n        sample = json.load(f)\\n    available = list(sample[\\\"benchmarks\\\"].keys())\\n    benchmarks = [b for b in {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nRRF (Reciprocal Rank Fusion) aggregation of contamination signals.\\n\\nFuses 4 per-item signal scores into a single exposure score using RRF\\n(Cormack et al. 2009). Also implements ablation variants: equal-weight\\nrank averaging and single-signal baselines.\\n\\nInput:  Per-item signal scores (JSON from compute_signals.py)\\nOutput: Per-model-item fused exposure scores + signal survival AUC matrix\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSIGNAL_KEYS = [\\\"s1_embedding\\\", \\\"s2_minkpp\\\", \\\"s3_constat\\\", \\\"s4_selfcritique\\\"]\\nRRF_K = 60\\n\\n\\ndef detect_available_signals(items: list[dict], candidates: list[str] = None) -> list[str]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Auto-detect which signals have non-trivial values (not all null/zero).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if candidates is None:\\n        candidates = SIGNAL_KEYS\\n    available = []\\n    for sig in candidates:\\n        vals = [item.get(sig) for item in items]\\n        if any(v is not None and v != 0.0 for v in vals):\\n            available.append(sig)\\n    return available\\n\\n\\ndef load_signal_data(signal_files: list[str]) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load and merge signal files. Each file contains a list of per-item dicts.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    merged = {}\\n    for fpath in signal_files:\\n        with open(fpath) as f:\\n            data = json.load(f)\\n        for item in data:\\n            idx = item[\\\"index\\\"]\\n            if idx not in merged:\\n                merged[idx] = {\\\"index\\\": idx, \\\"benchmark\\\": item.get(\\\"benchmark\\\", \\\"\\\")}\\n            merged[idx].update(item)\\n    return [merged[k] for k in sorted(merged)]\\n\\n\\ndef compute_ranks(scores: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Rank items from 1 (highest score = most suspicious) to N.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    order = np.argsort(-scores)  # descending\\n    ranks = np.empty_like(order)\\n    ranks[order] = np.arange(1, len(scores) + 1)\\n    return ranks\\n\\n\\ndef rrf_aggregate(items: list[dict], k: int = RRF_K,\\n                  signals: list[str] = None) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    RRF fusion: score_j = sum_s 1/(k + rank_s(j))\\n\\n    All signals are oriented so higher = more suspicious.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if signals is None:\\n        signals = detect_available_signals(items)\\n        if not signals:\\n            logger.warning(\\\"No signals with non-trivial values found\\\")\\n            return []\\n\\n    n = len(items)\\n    if n == 0:\\n        return []\\n\\n    #       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nRRF (Reciprocal Rank Fusion) aggregation of contamination signals.\\n\\nFuses 4 per-item signal scores into a single exposure score using RRF\\n(Cormack et al. 2009). Also implements ablation variants: equal-weight\\nrank averaging and single-signal baselines.\\n\\nInput:  Per-item signal scores (JSON from compute_signals.py)\\nOutput: Per-model-item fused exposure scores + signal survival AUC matrix\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSIGNAL_KEYS = [\\\"s1_embedding\\\", \\\"s2_minkpp\\\", \\\"s3_constat\\\", \\\"s4_selfcritique\\\"]\\nRRF_K = 60\\n\\n\\ndef detect_available_signals(items: list[dict], candidates: list[str] = None) -> list[str]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Auto-detect which signals have non-trivial values (not all null/zero).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if candidates is None:\\n        candidates = SIGNAL_KEYS\\n    available = []\\n    for sig in candidates:\\n        vals = [item.get(sig) for item in items]\\n        if any(v is not None and v != 0.0 for v in vals):\\n            available.append(sig)\\n    return available\\n\\n\\ndef load_signal_data(signal_files: list[str]) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load and merge signal files. Each file contains a list of per-item dicts.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    merged = {}\\n    for fpath in signal_files:\\n        with open(fpath) as f:\\n            data = json.load(f)\\n        for item in data:\\n            idx = item[\\\"index\\\"]\\n            if idx not in merged:\\n                merged[idx] = {\\\"index\\\": idx, \\\"benchmark\\\": item.get(\\\"benchmark\\\", \\\"\\\")}\\n            merged[idx].update(item)\\n    return [merged[k] for k in sorted(merged)]\\n\\n\\ndef compute_ranks(scores: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Rank items from 1 (highest score = most suspicious) to N.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    order = np.argsort(-scores)  # descending\\n    ranks = np.empty_like(order)\\n    ranks[order] = np.arange(1, len(scores) + 1)\\n    return ranks\\n\\n\\ndef rrf_aggregate(items: list[dict], k: int = RRF_K,\\n                  signals: list[str] = None) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    RRF fusion: score_j = sum_s 1/(k + rank_s(j))\\n\\n    All signals are oriented so higher = more suspicious.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if signals is None:\\n        signals = detect_available_signals(items)\\n        if not signals:\\n            logger.warning(\\\"No signals with non-trivial values found\\\")\\n            return []\\n\\n    n = len(items)\\n    if n == 0:\\n        return []\\n\\n    # {
```
```json
  "server": "cqa1-21977",
```
```json
  "command": "source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python -c \"\nimport json, numpy as np\n\n# Simulate exp-006 load_exposure_scores\nwith open('/tmp/rrf_dryrun/exposure_scores.json') as f:\n    raw = json.load(f)\n\nbenchmarks_data = {\n    'mmlu': {'n_items': 14042},\n    'gsm8k': {'n_items': 1319},\n    'arc_challenge': {'n_items': 1172},\n}\n\nexposure = {}\nfor bench in benchmarks_data:\n    if bench in raw:\n        exposure[bench] = np.array(raw[bench], dtype=float)\n    else:\n        exposure[bench] = np.ones(benchmarks_data[bench]['n_items'])\n\nfor b, arr in exposure.items():\n    print(f'{b}: shape={arr.shape}, dtype={arr.dtype}, range=[{arr.min():.6f}, {arr.max():.6f}]')\n    assert len(arr) == benchmarks_data[b]['n_items'], f'Length mismatch: {len(arr)} vs {benchmarks_data[b][\\\"n_items\\\"]}'\n\n# Test build_exposure_matrix\ndosages = np.array([0, 5, 100])\nfor b, tau in exposure.items():\n    max_d = dosages.max()\n    matrix = np.outer(dosages / max_d, tau)\n    print(f'{b}: exposure_matrix shape={matrix.shape}, range=[{matrix.min():.6f}, {matrix.max():.6f}]')\n\nprint('COMPATIBILITY OK')\n\""
}
```

## 报告

### 代码审查结果

**aggregate_rrf.py** — 3个问题，已全部修复：

| # | 问题 | 严重度 | 状态 |
|---|------|--------|------|
| 1 | **输出格式不兼容 exp-006** — 输出 `[{index, benchmark, rrf_score}]` 但 exp-006 的 `load_exposure_scores` 期望 `{benchmark: [float]}` | Critical | 已修复：新增 `--generate-exposure` 模式和 `generate_exposure_json()` 函数 |
| 2 | **缺失信号自动检测** — ConStat(s3) 未计算时全为 0.0，产生基于 index 顺序的系统性 rank 偏差 | High | 已修复：新增 `detect_available_signals()`，RRF/rank-avg 自动跳过全零信号 |
| 3 | **run_batch.py 跳过检测失效** — `merged_output_exists` 查找 `signals_merged_{bench}_{model_id}.json`，但 compute_signals.py 写的是 `signals_merged_{bench}.json` | Medium | 已修复 |

### 输入/输出格式兼容性

- compute_signals.py → `signal_{name}_{bench}.json` + `signals_merged_{bench}.json` ✅ aggregate_rrf.py 的 `load_signal_data` 可读取
- aggregate_rrf.py `--generate-exposure` → `exposure_scores.json` 格式 `{benchmark: [float]}` ✅ 与 exp-006 `load_exposure_scores` 完全匹配
- 验证通过：14042 MMLU + 1319 GSM8K + 1172 ARC items，`build_exposure_matrix` 产出形状正确

### Dry-run 结果

用 d000 已有的 embedding 信号跑通两种模式：
- 单模型 RRF：14042 items，正确检测到仅 s1_embedding 可用
- exposure 生成：3 个 benchmark 全部产出，tau 范围 [0, 1]（min-max 归一化后）

### exp-002 完成后的运行命令

```bash
cd /root/contamcal-irt-calibration/exp002
python aggregate_rrf.py \
  --generate-exposure \
  --signal-dir /root/autodl-tmp/exp002/signals \
  --output-dir /root/autodl-tmp/exp003/output \
  --dosage-filter d100 \
  --benchmarks mmlu gsm8k arc_challenge
```

建议用 `--dosage-filter d100` — d100 模型所有 benchmark items 都在训练集中，信号最强，tau 估计最可靠。
